Result: 指出群论在粒子物理中具有特殊重要性，在统计物理中亦不可忽视，同时对理论生物学可能也具有研究价值。

Conclusion: 群论不仅在现代数学中发展显著，更在多个科学领域（物理学、生物学等）展现出广泛而深远的影响力，值得进一步探索其跨学科应用。

Abstract: The development of Group Theory in Mathematics as well as its impact in Physics has been spectacular during the twentieth century, and more particularly these last fifty years. If its contribution to Particle Physics deserves a special consideration, its usefulness cannot be neglected in other domains like Statistical Physics for example, and might also be of interest in Theoretical Biology. Some of these aspects will be examined.

</details>


### [130] [Stress stability criterion of the isospinning $\mathbb{C}P^2$ solitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04413)
*Sergei Antsipovich,Yakov Shnir*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了(2+1)维$\mathbb{C}P^2$非线性sigma模型中稳态旋转拓扑孤子的能量-动量张量，评估了剪切力和压力分布，发现孤子在特定参数范围内存在经典不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究稳态旋转拓扑孤子的能量-动量张量及其稳定性，以理解这类孤子在非线性sigma模型中的物理行为。

Method: 通过计算(2+1)维$\mathbb{C}P^2$非线性sigma模型（含稳定势项）中拓扑孤子的能量-动量张量，分析剪切力和压力的空间分布，并基于这些分布评估孤子的经典稳定性。

Result: 孤子在系统参数的某些取值范围内表现出经典不稳定性。

Conclusion: 稳态旋转$\mathbb{C}P^2$拓扑孤子的稳定性强烈依赖于系统参数，存在导致经典不稳定的参数区间。

Abstract: We study the energy-momentum tensor of stationary rotating topological solitons in a (2 + 1)-dimensional $\mathbb{C}P^2$ nonlinear sigma model with a stabilizing potential term. We evaluate the distributions of the corresponding shear forces and pressure and study the stability criteria for these solutions. Our results suggest that these solitons become classically unstable for some range of values of the parameters of the system.

</details>


### [131] [Neumann scalars in AdS: partition functions and phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04417)
*Biki Bishwakarma,Astha Kakkar,Swarnendu Sarkar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文分析了AdS_{d+1}中满足Neumann边界条件的标量场单圈配分函数，通过从Dirichlet边界条件对应的Δ_+到Neumann边界条件对应的Δ_-的解析延拓实现计算，并对比了两种边界条件下的相位特征，通过关联函数的长程行为佐证了这些相位的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展对AdS中标量场边界条件的研究，从Dirichlet边界条件推广到Neumann边界条件，探索不同边界条件下标量场理论的相位结构及其在零温和有限温下的行为。

Method: 通过解析延拓从Dirichlet边界条件的Δ_+到Neumann边界条件的Δ_-来计算单圈配分函数；通过变形Laplace算子本征值λ的积分轮廓实现计算；通过分析关联函数的长程行为验证相位的存在。

Result: 成功实现了Neumann边界条件下标量场在AdS_{d+1}中的单圈配分函数计算，并与Dirichlet边界条件的结果进行了对比，发现了不同的相位结构。

Conclusion: Neumann边界条件与Dirichlet边界条件导致不同的相位特征，这些相位可以通过关联函数的长程行为加以区分和验证。

Abstract: We present an analysis of one-loop partition functions for scalars in AdS$_{d+1}$ obeying the Neumann boundary condition and explore phases of scalar field theories in several dimensions at zero and finite temperature. The partition function computation involves an analytic continuation from $Δ_+$ corresponding to the Dirichlet boundary condition to $Δ_-$ corresponding to Neumann boundary condition. We show that this can be implemented by deformations of the contour for integral over eigenvalue ($λ$) of the Laplace operator as compared to the integral over ${\mathbb R}$ in [arXiv:2201.09043] for the Dirichlet boundary condition. We further contrast these phases with those appearing for the case of scalars obeying the Dirichlet boundary condition and corroborate the occurrence of these phases by studying the long range behaviour of correlators.

</details>


### [132] [Topological effects in the polarization of the Fulling-Rindler vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04483)
*A. A. Saharian,G. V. Mirzoyan,V. S. Torosyan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了Rindler时空中某些空间维度的紧致化如何影响处于Fulling-Rindler真空态的带电标量场的场平方和能量-动量张量的真空期望值(VEVs)。采用环面紧致化，沿紧致维度对场算符施加准周期条件，这些条件的相位解释为紧致维度所包围的磁通量。对于一般空间维度数，VEV的分量被明确分离，对应于Minkowski真空中的期望值。作为极限情况，在平凡拓扑的Rindler时空恢复了VEVs。结果表明，对于非零相位，真空能量-动量张量在紧致子空间中存在非对角分量。在Rindler视界附近，场平方和对角能量-动量张量的渐近展开主导项与平凡拓扑Rindler时空中的对应VEV一致，而非对角分量在视界上消失。在小加速条件下，Fulling-Rindler与Minkowski真空的VEV差异呈指数小，但质量为零且相位为零的场例外，其差异随加速呈幂律衰减。应用方面，考虑了圆柱形黑洞和具有环形视界的拓扑黑洞视界附近的VEVs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Rindler时空中紧致化额外维度及准周期边界条件（相位对应磁通量）对量子真空涨落的影响，理解非平凡拓扑和磁场环境下加速观测者所见的真空结构。

Method: 采用环面紧致化方法，对场算符施加准周期边界条件（相位对应磁通量），在Fulling-Rindler真空中对带电标量场的场平方和能量-动量张量进行模式求和与正则化计算，分离出Minkowski真空部分，并分析渐近行为。

Result: (1) 非零相位下能量-动量张量在紧致子空间出现非对角分量；(2) 视界附近对角分量与平凡拓扑一致，非对角分量在视界上消失；(3) 小加速下VEV差异指数小，但零质量零相位场呈幂律衰减。

Conclusion: 紧致维度和磁通量会显著改变Rindler时空中的真空期望值，产生非对角能量-动量张量分量，且视界附近的渐近行为与拓扑无关；这些结果可应用于圆柱形黑洞和环形视界拓扑黑洞的量子效应研究。

Abstract: We investigate how the compactification of some spatial dimensions in Rindler spacetime affects the vacuum expectation values (VEVs) of the field squared and the energy-momentum tensor for a charged scalar field prepared in the Fulling-Rindler vacuum state. A toral compactification is considered with quasi-periodicity conditions on the field operator along the compact dimensions. The phases of these conditions are interpreted in terms of the magnetic flux enclosed by the compact dimensions. For a general number of spatial dimensions, the components of the VEVs are explicitly separated, corresponding to the expectation values in the Minkowski vacuum. As a limiting case, the VEVs are retrieved in Rindler spacetime with trivial topology. We demonstrate that, for non-zero phases in the periodicity conditions, the vacuum energy-momentum tensor exhibits off-diagonal components with indices in the compact subspace. Near the Rindler horizon, the leading terms in the asymptotic expansions of the field squared and the diagonal components of the energy-momentum tensor coincide with the corresponding VEVs in Rindler spacetime with trivial topology. The off-diagonal components of the energy-momentum tensor vanish on the Rindler horizon. For small accelerations, the difference between the VEVs in the Fulling-Rindler and Minkowski vacua is exponentially small. The exception to this is a massless field with zero phases in the periodicity conditions. In this special case, the difference decays according to a power law as a function of acceleration. As an application, we consider the VEVs near the horizon of cylindrical black holes and topological black holes with toroidal horizons.

</details>


### [133] [Kaluza-Klein tower thresholds and scheme dependence of the species scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04602)
*Yuri Michinobu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了量子引力中的“物种标度”（species scale），阐明了两种常用定义（基于微扰失效能量 vs. 基于高阶导数算符的压制标度）之间的关系。作者发现，一圈塔贡献中领先的局域修正依赖于截断方案，应视为EFT匹配数据，而次领头对数项在特定类正规化截断下是普适的。以Kaluza-Klein塔为例，文章分离了与正规化无关的对数信息与方案依赖的局域阈值贡献，解释了为何两种定义的系数级识别并非正规化无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子引力中物种标度有两种常见但关系不明确的定义：一是从引力子传播子一圈修正导出的微扰失效能量，二是引力有效作用量中高阶导数算符的压制标度。本文旨在澄清这两种定义之间的关系，特别是通过分析截断方案依赖性，解释为何两者在系数层面不能简单地等同。

Method: 从EFT视角出发，系统分析一圈塔贡献对截断方案的依赖性。采用proper-time截断类，区分领先的塔增强局域修正（方案依赖，视为EFT匹配数据）与次领头对数项（普适）。以Kaluza-Klein塔为具体实例，计算完整的四导数一圈修正，分离正规化稳定的对数信息与方案依赖的局域阈值贡献。

Result: 证明了领先的塔增强局域修正是截断方案依赖的，应被解释为EFT匹配数据而非普适定义；次领头对数项在考虑的proper-time截断类中是普适的。通过KK塔实例展示了如何分离这两类贡献，从而建立了两种物种标度定义之间的可控EFT联系。

Conclusion: 物种标度的微扰失效定义与高阶导数压制定义之间的系数级识别并非正规化无关，必须谨慎处理方案依赖性。正确理解这一关系需要区分普适的对数信息与方案依赖的局域阈值贡献，这为量子引力中物种标度的EFT解释提供了更清晰的理论基础。

Abstract: We revisit the species scale in quantum gravity from the viewpoint of effective field theory (EFT). Two characterizations are commonly used: one defines it as the energy at which the perturbative description of quantum gravity breaks down, as inferred from the one-loop correction to the graviton propagator; the other identifies it with the suppression scale of higher-derivative operators in the gravitational effective action. We clarify the relation between these characterizations by analyzing the cutoff-scheme dependence of the one-loop tower contribution. For a large number of species, the leading tower-enhanced local correction is regulator dependent and should be interpreted as EFT matching data, while the subleading logarithmic contribution is universal within the class of proper-time cutoff profiles considered here. As a concrete application, we compute the full four-derivative one-loop corrections from Kaluza-Klein towers. Our results separate regulator-stable logarithmic data from scheme-dependent local threshold contributions, providing a controlled EFT interpretation of the relation between perturbative-breakdown and higher-derivative definitions of the species scale and explaining why their coefficient-level identification is not regulator independent.

</details>


### [134] [Multi-Particle Contributions to the Celestial Algebra in the ${\cal N}=8$ Supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04611)
*Changhyun Ahn*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文将Calkins和Pate提出的多粒子OPE构造方法推广到${\cal N}=8$超引力理论中，计算了引力子、引力微子、引力光子、引力光微子及标量粒子的多粒子OPE，通过围道积分得到95个（反）对易子，并建立了四维体中玻色子与费米子的三共线极限关系，同时隐含提出了单粒子天球算符与多粒子天球算符的一般OPE形式及其对应模的（反）对易子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Calkins和Pate在纯爱因斯坦引力中建立的多粒子OPE构造方法推广到更一般的${\cal N}=8$超引力理论，以研究该理论中各类粒子的天球软算子代数结构及其对应的散射振幅关系。

Method: 基于Calkins和Pate在纯引力中的多粒子OPE构造，将其推广到${\cal N}=8$超引力中涉及引力子、引力微子、引力光子、引力光微子及标量粒子的情形；通过围道积分从多粒子OPE中提取出单粒子贡献的（反）对易子；利用四维体中的三共线极限进行交叉验证。

Result: 得到了95个多粒子OPE及其对应的（反）对易子；建立了这些OPE与天球振幅之间的非平凡关系；通过三共线极限验证了结果的一致性。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将多粒子OPE构造推广到${\cal N}=8$超引力，得到了丰富的天球软代数结构，并隐含提出了更一般的$N$粒子情形的OPE形式及含线性项（单粒子交换项）的（反）对易子关系，为高维超引力中的天球全息对偶研究提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Recently, Calkins and Pate calculated the multi-particle operator product expansions (OPEs) of single-particle celestial operators with two-particle celestial operators in pure Einstein gravity. We apply their construction to the graviton, gravitinos, graviphotons, graviphotinos and scalars in ${\cal N}=8$ supergravity. By performing various contour integrals on these multi-particle OPEs, we obtain ninety-five (anti)commutators for the single-particle contributions from the celestial soft current algebra (derived from the single-particle OPEs) in a two-dimensional boundary. Moreover, these ninety-five multi-particle OPEs provide nontrivial relations between the corresponding celestial amplitudes. The triple-collinear limits between these bosonic and fermionic particles from the four-dimensional bulk are also described as a double check. Finally, we implicitly propose both the multi-particle OPEs of single-particle celestial operators with $(N-1)$-particle celestial operators and the corresponding (anti)commutators for the modes of celestial operators that have linear terms (or single-particle exchange terms due to the sum of $N$-particle factorization channels from amplitudes) on the right-hand sides.

</details>


### [135] [New insights on mutual information in the island approach to the Page curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04706)
*Anirban Roy Chowdhury,Souvik Paul,Sunandan Gangopadhyay*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发现了Page时间后不同子系统互信息的关键行为：$I(B_+:B_-)$在 scrambling 时间归零，而 $I(\mathcal{I}:R)$ 趋于无穷，表明几何纠缠守恒；并首次提出了 Cauchy 切片上三方互信息 $I(\mathcal{I}:R_+:R_-)$ 的计算方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Page时间后不同子系统互信息的行为，揭示几何纠缠的守恒性质，并填补三方互信息计算的空白。

Method: 通过分析永恒黑洞的Page曲线，研究两个特定子系统间的互信息（$I(B_+:B_-)$和$I(\mathcal{I}:R)$）在scrambling时间的行为，并利用双区域结果推导Cauchy切片上的三方互信息$I(\mathcal{I}:R_+:R_-)$。

Result: 1) $I(B_+:B_-)$在scrambling时间归零，表明$B_+$和$B_-$间无关联；2) $I(\mathcal{I}:R)$在同一时间趋于无穷，表明$\mathcal{I}$和$R$达到最大纠缠；3) 首次给出了三方互信息$I(\mathcal{I}:R_+:R_-)$的计算方法。

Conclusion: 不同区域间的互信息在特定时间点的互补行为体现了几何关联的守恒，而三方互信息的计算完善了对Cauchy切片上纠缠结构的理解。

Abstract: In this article, we have presented one of the very important observations, regarding the behavior of mutual information of two different sets of subsystems in the after Page time scenario. This provides us with some deep insights about the conservation of geometrical entanglement. In our earlier works, we have shown how the saturation of mutual information between two specific subsystems plays a vital role in obtaining the correct Page curve for the eternal black hole. In those works, we have shown that the mutual information between $B_+$ and $B_-$, that is, $I(B_{+}: B_{-})$, vanishes at scrambling time, which leads to the correct Page curve. That means that at scrambling time, there is no correlation between $B_+$ and $B_-$. Remarkably, it is observed that at this particular value of observer's time, the mutual information between $\mathcal{I}$ and $R$, that is, $I(\mathcal{I}:R)$, becomes infinity. This indicates that the regions $\mathcal{I}$ and $R$ become maximally entangled. This provides us with a notion of conservation of geometric correlation between different regions on the Cauchy slice. In this work, we have also provided a way to calculate the tripartite mutual information of regions $\mathcal{I}$,$R_+$ and $R_-$, that is, $I(\mathcal{I}:R_+:R_-)$ on the Cauchy slice using the earlier results involving the bipartite regions. This is a new result which was missing in the earlier literature.

</details>


### [136] [Revisiting the Wess-Zumino-Witten Term in Nuclear and Quark Matter under Magnetic Fields and Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04929)
*Markus A. G. Amano,Minoru Eto,Muneto Nitta,Shin Sasaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在强磁场和旋转条件下，致密QCD物质中与手征反常相关的反常Wess-Zumino-Witten项。通过引入电磁、重子数和同位旋背景规范场，推导了$N_f=2$和$N_f=3$情况下中性介子的拓扑耦合项，得到了与$\vec{B} \cdot \vec{\nabla} φ$和$\vecΩ \cdot \vec{\nabla} φ$成正比的特征贡献，这些结果与致密旋转物质中的手征孤子晶格相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在强磁场和旋转条件下致密QCD物质中的反常Wess-Zumino-Witten项，以理解手征反常在极端环境下的拓扑贡献及其与中性介子的耦合机制。

Method: 引入电磁、重子数和同位旋背景规范场，针对$N_f=2$和$N_f=3$两种情况，推导中性介子（$π^0$、$η$、$η'$）的拓扑耦合项，分析磁场和旋转对反常WZW项的影响。

Result: 得到了包含$\vec{B} \cdot \vec{\nabla} φ$和$\vecΩ \cdot \vec{\nabla} φ$特征贡献的拓扑耦合项，揭示了磁场梯度和旋转梯度与中性介子场的耦合关系。

Conclusion: 这些发现对理解致密旋转物质中的手征孤子晶格具有重要意义，为极端条件下QCD物质的手征性质提供了新的理论见解。

Abstract: We study anomalous Wess-Zumino-Witten terms associated with the chiral anomaly in dense QCD matter under magnetic fields and rotation. By introducing electromagnetic, baryon number, and isospin background gauge fields, we write down the topological couplings of neutral mesons for the $N_f=2$ and $N_f=3$ cases. The resulting terms contain characteristic contributions proportional to $\vec{B} \cdot \vec{\nabla} φ$ and $\vecΩ \cdot \vec{\nabla} φ$, where $φ$ denotes $π^0$, $η$, or $η'$. These results are relevant to chiral soliton lattices in dense rotating matter.

</details>


### [137] [The causal set reduction formula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04980)
*Stav Zalel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在因果集背景下推导了矩阵元的约化公式，以及类似Schwinger-Dyson方程的关联函数无穷级数关系，从而将矩阵元表达为路径积分和两种不同的关联函数求和形式。该方法绕过了显式微分运动方程算子，并在平坦连续时空下为标准LSZ结果提供了替代表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在因果集（causal set）这一离散时空框架下，缺乏传统的微分运动方程算符，因此需要发展新的方法来计算矩阵元。作者旨在建立一套不依赖于连续微分算子、而能在因果集背景下直接计算散射矩阵元的方法。

Method: 推导了因果集背景下的矩阵元约化公式；建立了类似于Schwinger-Dyson方程的关联函数无穷级数关系；将矩阵元表达为路径积分形式和两种不同的关联函数求和形式。该方法绕过了显式微分运动方程算子，并在平坦连续时空极限下与标准LSZ公式建立了联系。

Result: 得到了矩阵元的三种等价表达形式：路径积分形式和两种不同的关联函数求和形式。该方法在平坦连续时空下提供了标准LSZ结果的替代表达式。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一套在因果集离散时空背景下计算矩阵元的系统方法，绕过了传统连续时空中的微分算子，并且与标准LSZ公式相兼容，为量子引力中的散射计算提供了新工具。

Abstract: We derive a reduction formula for matrix elements on a causal set background. We derive an infinite tower of relations between correlators, akin to the Schwinger-Dyson equations of the continuum. Combining these two results we are able to express matrix elements in three different forms: as a path integral and as two distinct sums of correlators. We sketch the form that our method - which circumvents explicit use of differential equation of motion operators - takes in flat continuum spacetime where it provides an alternative expression for the standard LSZ result.

</details>


### [138] [From Galilei to Euclidean Carroll and the Alice Particle: The Times They Are a-Changin'](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05115)
*Eric A. Bergshoeff,Luca Romano,Jan Rosseel,Elena Simón Félix,Sara Zeko*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一方法来处理Galilei和Carroll极限，通过将p-膜Galilei极限中的世界体积变为欧几里得，得到“欧几里得p-膜Carroll极限”。针对p=0的情况，发现欧几里得0-膜Carroll极限产生的时空对称性可中心扩展为Alice代数，类比Bargmann代数扩展Galilei对称性。由此引入Alice粒子概念，并从相对论性有质量粒子或快子的统一极限中导出Bargmann和Alice粒子作用量。在宇宙学常数存在时，Bargmann和Alice粒子分别在负和正宇宙学常数下稳定运动。两种粒子作用量均可通过零约化从单时间或双时间相对论时空中的无质量粒子作用量得到。在10维中，Alice粒子对应于Hull的IIA*理论中D0*-膜的退耦极限，类似Bargmann粒子与IIA弦理论中D0-膜的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立Galilei极限与Carroll极限的统一框架，探索p-膜Galilei极限向Carroll极限的转化机制，并揭示由此产生的新对称性结构及物理粒子（Alice粒子）的性质。

Method: 将p-膜Galilei极限中的世界体积从洛伦兹型改为欧几里得型，使时间变为横向，从而将极限转化为Carroll型极限（欧几里得p-膜Carroll极限）。针对p=0情形，从相对论性有质量粒子或快子的作用量出发，通过统一极限耦合规范场，导出Bargmann和Alice粒子作用量。利用零约化方法从高维无质量粒子作用量导出低维作用量，并在10维超弦理论中给出物理解释。

Result: 建立了Galilei与Carroll极限的统一处理方式；发现了Alice代数这一新的对称性代数结构；提出了Alice粒子的概念；给出了Bargmann和Alice粒子在宇宙学常数下的稳定运动条件；通过零约化建立了与高维时空的联系；在10维弦论中识别了Alice粒子为D0*-膜的退耦极限。

Conclusion: 欧几里得p-膜Carroll极限提供了一种将Galilei和Carroll极限统一处理的新框架，Alice代数及其对应的Alice粒子丰富了非相对论极限理论，并在弦论中有自然实现。

Abstract: In generalized, also known as $p$-brane Galilei limits, the speed of light $c$ becomes infinite in the directions transverse to a $(p+1)$-dimensional Lorentzian worldvolume. In this paper, we explain that allowing the worldvolume to be Euclidean, and thus time to be transversal, $p$-brane Galilei limits turn into Carrollian ones, that we refer to as "Euclidean $p$-brane Carroll limits", in which $c$ goes to zero in the $p+1$ worldvolume directions. This leads to a unified approach to taking Galilean and Carrollian limits, whose consequences we explore for $p=0$. We show that the spacetime symmetries that arise from the Euclidean 0-brane Carroll limit can be centrally extended to what we will call the Alice algebra, similar to how the Bargmann algebra centrally extends the Galilei symmetries. This gives rise to the novel notion of an Alice particle, and we obtain the Bargmann and Alice particle actions from a unified limit of the action of a relativistic massive particle or tachyon, suitably coupled to a one-form gauge potential. In the presence of a cosmological constant, we find that the Bargmann and Alice particles undergo stable motion for negative and positive cosmological constant, respectively. Finally, we show that the Bargmann and Alice particle actions can be obtained from null reduction of a massless particle action in a relativistic spacetime with one and two times. Our results indicate that in 10 dimensions, the Alice particle is a decoupling limit of a D$0{}^*$-brane in Hull's type IIA${}^*$ theory, similar to how the Bargmann particle is related to a D$0$-brane in type IIA string theory.

</details>


### [139] [Chern Character for Discrete Spectrum Partition Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05136)
*Shunrui Li,Yang Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文建立了离散谱量子系统热配分函数与时空上“虚拟物理层”陈特征之间的严格几何对应。通过将哈密顿动力学解释为量子相空间CP^n上的U(1)-等变流并推前到时空，证明有限温配分函数可表示为时空上陈特征的积分。利用Grothendieck-Riemann-Roch形式体系，证明了热紧化下陈特征的推前不变性，为量子场论中的热迹提供了拓扑基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为量子系统的热配分函数建立严格的几何与拓扑基础，统一谱理论与示性类理论，从算子代数视角为热迹提供几何解释。

Method: 将哈密顿动力学解释为CP^n上的U(1)-等变流，通过推前构造时空上的“虚拟物理层”；利用Weyl渐近律保证迹类性并延拓至无穷维；运用Grothendieck-Riemann-Roch形式体系证明热紧化下的陈特征推前不变性。

Result: 证明了有限温配分函数等价于时空上虚拟物理层陈特征的积分；建立了热紧化下陈特征的推前不变性，为热迹提供了拓扑不变性基础。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了谱理论与示性类理论，为基于算子代数的量子场论热配分函数提供了严格的几何拓扑解释，建立了热力学与拓扑学之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We establish a rigorous geometric correspondence between thermal partition functions of discrete-spectrum quantum systems with bounded ground energy and the Chern character of "virtual physical sheaf" over spacetime. By interpreting Hamiltonian dynamics as a $U(1)$-equivariant flow on the quantum phase space $\mathbb{CP}^n$ and pushforward to spacetime, we show that the finite-temperature partition function emerges as the integral of the Chern character of "virtual physical sheaf" over spacetime. The construction extends naturally to infinite dimensions through trace class guaranteed by Weyl's aspmtotic law. Using the Grothendieck-Riemann-Roch formalism, we prove pushforward invariance of the Chern character under thermal compactification on arbitrary manifolds, providing a topological foundation for thermal traces in quantum field theory. This framework unifies spectral theory with characteristic class theory, offering a geometric interpretation of partition functions based on operator-algebraic approach.

</details>


### [140] [Thermal two-point functions in SYK and complex-time singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05258)
*Ilija Burić,Chi-Ming Chang,Ivan Gusev,Elizabeth Helfenberger,Andrei Parnachev,Mukund Rangamani*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过分析大N SYK模型在有限温度下的两点关联函数，研究了其在复时间平面上的解析结构。研究发现主导奇点位于虚轴上，定义了关联函数的"有效温度"并控制算符复杂度的短时动力学；次主导奇点位于该有效温度设定的热条带之外，可能与涌现黑洞几何中的弹跳零测地线相关。两种奇点在零温下仍然存在，作者从运动学空间视角讨论了涌现几何的动机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索SYK模型在远离红外不动点的中等耦合区域下，有限温度两点关联函数的复时间解析结构，以理解算符复杂度的动力学行为及其与涌现黑洞几何的关联。

Method: 对大N SYK模型在中等耦合强度下进行有限温度两点关联函数的解析结构分析，追踪复时间平面上奇点随温度的变化，并采用运动学空间视角解释涌现几何。

Result: 主导奇点位于虚轴上，定义了关联函数的"有效温度"并控制短时算符复杂度动力学；次主导奇点位于有效温度热条带之外，可能与黑洞几何中的弹跳零测地线相关；两种奇点均在零温下持续存在。

Conclusion: SYK模型在中等耦合下的复时间解析结构揭示了与涌现黑洞几何的深层联系，次主导奇点可能对应弹跳零测地线，为理解全息对偶提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We analyze the finite-temperature two-point function of the large-$N$ SYK model at intermediate couplings away from the infrared fixed point. Specifically, we examine its analytic structure in the complex time plane, tracking the complex-time singularities over a range of temperatures. The location of the leading singularity lies on the imaginary axis. It controls the short-time dynamics of operator complexity, defining an `effective temperature' for the correlator. The next-to-leading singularity lies outside the thermal strip set by the above effective temperature. It has been argued that this could be interpreted in terms of bouncing null geodesics in the emergent black hole geometry. Both these singularities persist all the way down to zero temperature. We discuss our observations and motivate the related emergent geometry using a kinematic space perspective.

</details>


### [141] [Polynomial Initial-State Jumps and Christoffel Transforms in Krylov Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05294)
*Abhishek Chowdhury,Ajit Prasad Mahapatra*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子态Krylov复杂度中初始态依赖问题的精确解析框架，通过多项式滤波将参考初始态的Lanczos系数和Krylov基映射到新初始态，导出了有限和公式与Christoffel-Darboux核，并在多种典型系统（如Heisenberg-Weyl/Charlier振子、SU(2)/Krawtchouk自旋、紧束缚/Chebyshev链）中验证，同时推广到算子Krylov复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Krylov复杂度依赖于哈密顿量H和初始态|K₀⟩的配对，改变初始态（固定H）会完全改变Lanczos系数和Krylov基，缺乏一个精确的解析转化工具。

Method: 对归一化多项式滤波器|ψ_Q⟩=Q(H)|K₀⟩/√N_Q，利用滤波后谱测度的正多项式修正|Q(E)|²dμ(E)/N_Q，通过正交性将参考Fourier正交多项式矩的有限带转移转化为移位Krylov振幅，导出单个振幅和累积概率/扩展复杂度的Christoffel-Darboux核有限和公式，并涵盖重根、复种子系数、支集丢失和有限维终端商。

Result: 在三种典型Jacobi族（Charlier、Krawtchouk、Chebyshev）中验证了构造，以Hermite中心极限标度作为连续谱检验；建立了矩阵值母测度框架，其标量压缩恢复各移位问题；将固定内积极构造推广到算子Krylov复杂度（H→ℒ, |K₀⟩→O），多项式种子变为嵌套对易子后代Q(ℒ)O。

Conclusion: 提供了一个精确的相对演算：求解一个循环参考问题后，无需在原始Hilbert空间中重复Lanczos过程，即可生成一族多项式相关的初始态动力学。

Abstract: State Krylov, or spread, complexity is a property of a pair $(H,\ket{K_0})$ rather than of the Hamiltonian alone. Thus, changing the initial state at fixed $H$ generally changes the Lanczos coefficients and the ordered Krylov basis. We solve this relative initial-state problem for normalized polynomial filters, $\ket{ψ_Q}=Q(H)\ket{K_0}/\sqrt{N_Q}$ with $N_Q=\langle K_0|Q(H)^\dagger Q(H)|K_0\rangle$. The filtered spectral measure is the positive polynomial modification $|Q(E)|^2\mathrm dμ(E)/N_Q$, and orthogonality turns this measure change into a finite-band transfer from reference Fourier-orthogonal-polynomial moments to shifted Krylov amplitudes. We derive exact finite sums for individual amplitudes and projected Christoffel-Darboux kernels for cumulative probabilities and spread complexity. The formulae cover confluent roots, complex seed coefficients, support loss, and terminal quotients in finite dimensions. We evaluate the construction in three canonical Jacobi families, the Heisenberg--Weyl/Charlier oscillator, the compact $SU(2)$/Krawtchouk spin, and the constant-coefficient tight-binding/Chebyshev chain, with a Hermite central-limit scaling of Charlier as a continuous-spectrum check of this Christoffel jump machinery. Finite seed families are organized by a matrix-valued parent measure whose scalar compressions recover the individual shifted problems. The fixed-inner-product construction carries over to operator Krylov complexity after the replacement \(H\mapsto\mathcal L\) and \(\ket{K_0}\mapsto O\); polynomial seeds then become nested-commutator descendants \(Q(\mathcal L)O\). The result is an exact relative calculus in which a solved cyclic problem generates a family of polynomially related initial-state dynamics without repeating Lanczos in the original Hilbert space.

</details>


### [142] [Cosmological Correlators in KLF and the Double-Exchange](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05327)
*Nathan Belrhali,Arthur Poisson,Sébastien Renaux-Petel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了利用Kontorovich-Lebedev-Fourier (KLF)空间形式体系得到树层宇宙学关联函数级数表示的方法，将In-In嵌套时间积分转化为频率积分，并通过Lauricella函数分析顶点函数的解析性质，最终以双级数超几何函数形式简化了双交换图的解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了简化宇宙学关联函数的计算，避免求解复杂的微分方程，需要一种能够直接计算树层关联函数的解析方法，同时区分背景贡献和宇宙学对撞机信号贡献。

Method: 采用KLF空间形式体系，将In-In形式下的嵌套时间积分转化为频率积分；利用积分表示和Lauricella函数的级数表示推导传播子和顶点函数的解析性质（奇点位置、留数、大频率渐近行为）；通过频率积分直接计算树层关联函数。

Result: 获得了任意数量场顶点函数的奇点位置、留数及大频率渐近行为；完整计算了双交换图，结果以最多双级数超几何函数的形式表达，显著简化了关联函数的解析形式。

Conclusion: KLF形式体系提供了一种无需依赖微分方程即可直接计算树层宇宙学关联函数的有效途径，能够自然区分不同的物理贡献，并为解析计算提供了更简洁的表达形式。

Abstract: In this work, we present the procedure to find series representations of tree-level cosmological correlators using the Kontorovich-Lebedev-Fourier (KLF) space formalism. This framework allows us to trade the in-in nested time integrals for frequency integrals over rational propagators and vertex functions, which encode interactions among quantum fields on a de Sitter background. Because these functions are the key objects to understand in order to perform a diagrammatic computation, we derive their relevant analytic properties by using both their integral representation and series representation in terms of Lauricella functions. For a vertex involving any number of fields, we obtain the location of singularities, the corresponding residues and the large-frequency asymptotic behaviour. Gathering these properties at each frequency integration allows us to compute a tree-level correlator directly, without relying on the differential equations it satisfies. To illustrate this procedure, we provide a complete treatment of the double-exchange diagram. The computation naturally distinguishes the different physical contributions, whether to the background or to the cosmological collider signal. The newly derived result is expressed at most in terms of a double series over hypergeometric functions, which simplifies the analytical expression of the correlator.

</details>


### [143] [Does QFT make sense in non-integer dimensions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05338)
*Diego Delmastro,Yan-Yan Li,Dikshant Rathore*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了将QFT解析延拓到分数维度$d\in\mathbb C$的经典问题，发现QCD和QED等常见理论在复$d$平面上存在分支割线，且低能CFT中许多算子的OPE和标度维度会随$d$跳跃变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视将量子场论解析延拓到分数维度这一经典问题，揭示其中被忽视的解析结构复杂性。

Method: 通过分析QCD和QED在复维度平面上的解析行为，研究低能CFT中算子的OPE和标度维度随维度$d$的变化规律。

Result: 发现QCD和QED在复$d$平面上存在分支割线，且低能CFT中许多算子的OPE和标度维度随$d$呈现跳跃性变化。

Conclusion: 常见的QFT在解析延拓到分数维度时存在非平凡的分支割线结构，这影响了低能CFT中算子的性质。

Abstract: We revisit the old problem of analytically continuing QFTs to fractional dimension $d\in\mathbb C$. We observe that common theories like QCD and QED have branch cuts in the complex $d$ plane. In particular, many operators in their low-energy CFT have OPEs and scaling dimensions that jump as a function of $d$.

</details>


### [144] [Charge-Sector Construction of the Type-IIB Axion--Dilaton Wormhole Partition Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05385)
*Soo-Jong Rey*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了IIB型轴子-胀子虫洞配分函数，从电荷扇区数据出发，通过长距离鞍点计算得到双端算符系数矩阵，进而推导出标量系数并分析其满足的各种性质与约束，最终表明虫洞配分函数的θ依赖性是电荷扇区标量系数的傅里叶变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从电荷扇区数据出发构建Type-IIB轴子-胀子虫洞配分函数，并系统分析其系数满足的物理与数学约束。

Method: 通过在特定轴子电荷（等价于形式场通量扇区）中进行长距离鞍点计算，得到双端算符项及其系数矩阵C^{ij}_ν；利用约化数据b将矩阵转化为标量系数W_ν[b]，进而得到虫洞配分函数Z_wh(θ;b)=∑_νW_ν[b]e^{iνθ}。

Result: 导出了虫洞配分函数的表达式，并分析了系数W_ν[b]满足的性质：离散对称性协变性、相位、绝对界、矩正定性、未约化系数矩阵的柯西-施瓦茨不等式、复θ定义域、电荷晶格尾部以及稀薄贝塞尔/斯凯勒姆极限。

Conclusion: 虫洞配分函数的θ依赖性本质上是电荷扇区标量系数的傅里叶变换，为理解轴子-胀子虫洞的非微扰效应提供了系统框架。

Abstract: I construct the Type-IIB axion--dilaton wormhole partition function from charge-sector data. In a chosen axion charge, equivalently form-field flux sector, the long-distance saddle calculation supplies a two-end operator term with coefficient matrix \(C^{ij}_ν\). The labels \(i,j\) label end-insertion operators; the labels \(A,B\) label parent universes. Reduction data \(b\) convert this matrix into scalar coefficients \(W_ν[b]\). The wormhole partition function in the theta variable is \(Z_{\rm wh}(θ;b)=\sum_νW_ν[b]\e^{iνθ}\). I analyze properties and constraints this coefficients satisfy: discrete-symmetry covariance, phase, absolute bounds, moment positivity, Cauchy--Schwarz inequalities for the unreduced coefficient matrix, complex-\(θ\) domains, charge-lattice tails, and the dilute Bessel/Skellam limit. The \(θ\)-dependence of the wormhole partition function is the Fourier transform of the charge-sector scalar coefficients.

</details>


### [145] [Liénard--Wiechert fields in AdS and flat-space antipodal matching from geodesic-centered Coulombic data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05395)
*Sarthak Duary*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过几何方法推导了平坦时空和全局AdS时空中的Liénard–Wiechert场，强调了反极点匹配的起源。在平坦时空中，通过使用以源时空测地线为中心的坐标系，将匀速运动电荷的场简化为库仑场；在AdS中，将类似构造推广到沿测地线运动的情形，得到了库仑型Liénard–Wiechert场的闭式解，并证明了体场满足精确的反极点协变性，其零无穷远极限重现了平坦时空的反极点匹配关系。文章还给出了库仑种子场的镜像电荷解释，统一描述了库仑场、反极点匹配和AdS到平坦时空的极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目的是从几何角度阐明Liénard–Wiechert场中反极点匹配条件的起源，并将这一构造从平坦时空推广到全局AdS时空，以建立统一的几何图像。

Method: 采用几何方法：在平坦时空中，使用以源测地线为中心的坐标系，将匀速运动电荷的场转化为静止库仑场，再通过坐标变换得到非中心框架下的场；在AdS中，使用测地线适配坐标和嵌入空间不变量，从AdS中心的静态麦克斯韦解出发，构造沿测地线运动的库仑型Liénard–Wiechert场。

Result: 得到了全局AdS中库仑型Liénard–Wiechert场的闭式解析解（包括规范不变分量F_{ρτ}），证明了体场满足精确的反极点协变性，且其零无穷远极限重现了平坦时空的标准反极点匹配关系（I^+_-与I^-_+之间的库仑数据匹配）。

Conclusion: 反极点匹配可以通过坐标系的几何变换（从源静止系到非中心系）得到自然理解；AdS中的库仑型场与平坦时空的对应关系通过镜像电荷解释得到统一，为理解AdS/CFT中的边界条件提供了几何基础。

Abstract: We give a geometric derivation of Li'enard--Wiechert fields in flat space and in global AdS, emphasizing the origin of antipodal matching. In flat space, the field of a uniformly moving charge is obtained by using coordinates centered on the source timelike geodesic, where the charge is at rest and the solution is the ordinary Coulomb field. The nontrivial angular dependence of the leading data at null infinity arises only after rewriting this static solution in a non-centered frame, making the antipodal matching condition transparent. We extend the same construction to global AdS, where uniform motion is replaced by motion along a timelike geodesic. Starting from the static Maxwell solution at the AdS center, we use geodesic-adapted coordinates and embedding-space invariants to reconstruct the field in arbitrary global coordinates. This yields a closed-form AdS analogue of the Coulombic Li'enard--Wiechert field, including the gauge-invariant component $F_{ρτ}$. We show that the bulk field satisfies exact antipodal covariance, and that its null-fringe limit reproduces the standard flat-space antipodal matching relation between the Coulombic data at $\mathscr I^+*-$ and $\mathscr I^-*+$. We also describe an image-charge interpretation of the Coulomb seed, giving a unified picture of Coulombic fields, antipodal matching, and the AdS-to-flat-space limit.

</details>

### [146] [Reentrant localization transition, quantum butterfly and robust edge modes in aperiodic zig-zag ladder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03954)
*Sayan Bhattacharya,Rhiddha Acharjee,Atanu Nandy*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 准周期无序的紧束缚晶格中，通过引入长程非对角调制，可使局域化电子态再次退局域化，出现第二次量子相变，伴随多分形能谱和边缘态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究准周期无序紧束缚晶格中，在强无序导致局域化后，能否通过非对角调制重新实现电子态退局域化，探索第二次局域化-退局域化量子相变。

Method: 在准周期无序的低维紧束缚晶格中引入最小长程非对角调制，通过逆参与率(IPR)、本征谱分析以及量子动力学研究来表征局域化转变。

Result: 长程非对角调制可使本征谱在特定动力学参数组合下重新出现退局域化，形成第二次量子相变；存在单粒子迁移率边；能谱呈现多分形结构和量子蝴蝶图案，且带隙中存在鲁棒边缘模。

Conclusion: 准周期无序系统中，长程非对角调制可诱发第二次局域化-退局域化相变，丰富了无序系统中的量子相变图景。

Abstract: Low dimensional tight-binding lattices in presence of quasiperiodic disorder generally exhibits localization transition. The system supports diffusive modes upto a limiting strength of disorder and all the eigenstates become localized beyond that critical strength thereby quenching the kinetic signature of the wavepacket. However, moving away from this situation, we demonstrate that with minimal long-range off-diagonal modulation, the eigenspectrum again may offer delocalization of electronic states for some subtle combination of kinetic parameters of the Hamiltonian leading to a second quantum phase change. The localization transitions are associated with the obvious presence of single-particle mobility edges. Multifractal energy landscape also shows quantum butterfly pattern with the in-gap robust edge modes. The re-emerging localization transition is manifested through the evaluation of inverse participation ratio, eigenspectrum and a pertinent quantum dynamical study.

</details>


### [147] [Katsura-Nagaosa-Balatsky magnetoelectricity in molecular magnets: Bipartite entanglement transfer by means of rotating electric field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03965)
*Zhirayr Adamyan,Ani Chobanyan,Vadim Ohanyan,Azadeh Ghannadan,Jozef Strecka,Saeed Haddadi,Hamid Arian Zad*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有Katsura-Nagaosa-Balatsky (KNB)自旋-电场耦合机制的自旋1/2海森堡三聚体中的量子纠缠，发现外加电场能有效调控双体和多体纠缠，其中旋转平面电场可实现近乎理想的双体纠缠在不同自旋对之间的可控转移，为分子尺度量子纠缠的电学操控提供了新平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在分子纳米磁体中通过电场而非传统磁场来有效调控量子纠缠的可能性，特别是利用KNB机制实现纠缠的灵活操控与传递。

Method: 采用Katsura-Nagaosa-Balatsky (KNB)机制描述自旋诱导的电极化，在自旋1/2海森堡三聚体模型中同时施加外磁场和面内电场（可独立调节强度和方向），通过计算双体纠缠负度和多体纠缠来定量分析量子关联特性。

Result: (1) 电场耦合可显著增强或抑制双体纠缠；(2) 旋转平面电场可实现双体纠缠在不同自旋对之间的可控转移；(3) 在均匀交换和均匀KNB耦合的对称情形下，纠缠转移近乎理想，一对自旋的纠缠负度接近理论最大值而其余对同时消失；(4) 转移效率可通过交换相互作用、键几何构型和非均匀自旋-电场耦合进行调控。

Conclusion: 具有KNB自旋-电场耦合的分子纳米磁体为在分子尺度上实现量子纠缠的电学操控、导引和局域化提供了有前景的平台。

Abstract: We investigate quantum entanglement in a spin-1/2 Heisenberg trimer with spin-induced electric polarization described by the Katsura-Nagaosa-Balatsky (KNB) mechanism in the presence of external magnetic and electric fields. The electric field is assumed to lie in the plane of the magnetic ions, allowing its strength and orientation to be tuned independently. We analyze both bipartite and tripartite entanglement and demonstrate that the spin-electric-field coupling provides an efficient mechanism for controlling quantum correlations within the molecular nanomagnet. Depending on the electric-field parameters, the bipartite entanglement can be significantly enhanced or suppressed, while the multipartite entanglement exhibits a rich dependence on the microscopic spin-electric coupling. Most notably, we demonstrate that a rotating in-plane electric field of constant magnitude induces a controllable transfer of bipartite entanglement between different spin pairs. In the symmetric case of homogeneous exchange interactions and uniform KNB coupling, this transfer is found to be nearly ideal, with the bipartite negativity approaching its theoretical maximum for one spin pair while simultaneously vanishing for the remaining pairs. We show that the efficiency of the transfer can be tailored through the exchange interactions, bond geometry, and nonuniform spin-electric coupling. These results establish molecular nanomagnets with KNB spin-electric coupling as a promising platform for the electrical manipulation, steering, and localization of quantum entanglement at the molecular scale.

</details>
